       DATA DIVISION.   

       SUBSCHEMA SECTION.   
       INVOKE SUBSCHEMA PST100UCOBSU
           IN FILE SUBSCHEMAS OF SCHEMA PST100SCHEMA
           COPYING RECORDS    INTO COMMON   
           COPYING DATA-NAMES INTO COMMON   
           RUN-UNIT-ID          IS YMBPAX   
           OVERLAY  PAX-REC WITH PAX-CXF-REC
169014*                          PAX-FUT-REC
                                 PAX-VI-REC 
144449*    SAVE DATA INCLUDES RUN-UNIT QUICK-BEFORE-LOOKS   
144449     SAVE DATA INCLUDES DEFERRED-UPDATES  
           DMCA COMMON  
           ERROR RECOVERY IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
           ROLLBACK       IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

               VARYING WS-PXFR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
               WS-PXFR-SUB > RSP06-PXFR-CNT (RSP06-PAX-SUB).

           GO TO 0800-PROCESS-PAX-FEES-EXIT.

       0810-LOOP-PAX-FEES.  

           IF  ( MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND
                 WRITE-FX-REC )  OR 
               ( NOT MULTI-CURR-BOOK )  
               EVALUATE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) 
                   WHEN 'INTD'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT 
                   WHEN 'INTC'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT 
                   WHEN 'INTF'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT 
                   WHEN 'USTT'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-USTTA-TAX-AMT
                   WHEN 'CTFU'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-CITY-FUEL-AMT
                   WHEN 'CTFA'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-CITY-FACILITY-AMT
                   WHEN 'CTSE'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-AIR-SECURITY-TAX-AMT 
                   WHEN 'CSTM'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT  
                   WHEN 'IMGT'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-IMMIG-TAX-AMT
                   WHEN 'CRFU'  
                       ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
191200             WHEN 'APHI'  
191200                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
191200                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
191200                                   TO YMBPAX-APHIS-FEE-AMT
219347             WHEN 'CRAU'  
219347                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
219347                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
219347                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
219347             WHEN 'APMC'  
219347                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
219347                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
219347                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
219347             WHEN 'AMEL'  
219347                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
219347                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
219347                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
219347             WHEN 'ASYD'  
219347                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
219347                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
219347                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
CALFEE             WHEN 'GTFU'  
CALFEE                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
CALFEE                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
CALFEE                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
CALFEE             WHEN 'GTFA'  
CALFEE                 ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
CALFEE                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
CALFEE                                   TO YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE   
               END-EVALUATE 
           ELSE 
               EVALUATE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) 
                   WHEN 'INTD'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT ROUNDED =
                               YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT +
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'INTC'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT ROUNDED =
                               YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT +
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'INTF'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT ROUNDED =
                               YMBPAX-INTL-DEP-AMT +
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'USTT'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-USTTA-TAX-AMT ROUNDED =   
                               YMBPAX-USTTA-TAX-AMT +   
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'CTFU'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-CITY-FUEL-AMT ROUNDED =   
                               YMBPAX-CITY-FUEL-AMT +   
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'CTFA'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-CITY-FACILITY-AMT ROUNDED =   
                               YMBPAX-CITY-FACILITY-AMT +   
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'CTSE'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-AIR-SECURITY-TAX-AMT ROUNDED =
                               YMBPAX-AIR-SECURITY-TAX-AMT +
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'CSTM'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT ROUNDED = 
                               YMBPAX-CUSTOMS-FEE-AMT + 
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'IMGT'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-IMMIG-TAX-AMT ROUNDED =   
                               YMBPAX-IMMIG-TAX-AMT +   
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
                   WHEN 'CRFU'  
                       COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
                               YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
                               ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
                                 (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
                                 RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
191200             WHEN 'APHI'  
191200                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-APHIS-FEE-AMT  ROUNDED =  
191200                         YMBPAX-APHIS-FEE-AMT  +  
191200                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
191200                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
191200                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
219347             WHEN 'CRAU'  
219347                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
219347                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
219347                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
219347                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
219347                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
219347             WHEN 'APMC'  
219347                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
219347                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
219347                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
219347                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
219347                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
219347             WHEN 'AMEL'  
219347                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
219347                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
219347                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
219347                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
219347                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
219347             WHEN 'ASYD'  
219347                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
219347                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
219347                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
219347                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
219347                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
CALFEE             WHEN 'GTFU'  
CALFEE                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
CALFEE                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
CALFEE                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
CALFEE                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
CALFEE                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
CALFEE             WHEN 'GTFA'  
CALFEE                 COMPUTE YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE ROUNDED =  
CALFEE                         YMBPAX-GOVERNMENT-FEE +  
CALFEE                         ( RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT 
CALFEE                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) /  
CALFEE                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE )   
               END-EVALUATE 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-PXFR-SUB              TO YMBPXF-FEE-SUB. 
           MOVE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-CODE          (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPXF-TAX-FEE-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-GROUP-CODE    (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPXF-TAX-FEE-GROUP-CODE.  
           IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
               WRITE-FX-REC 
               MOVE RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPXF-TAX-FEE-AMT  
CCLAUS        MOVE RSP06-PDI-PXFR-FEE-GST-AMT(RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
CCLAUS                                   TO YMBPXF-PXFR-FEE-GST-AMT 
           ELSE 
              COMPUTE YMBPXF-TAX-FEE-AMT ROUNDED = RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT   
                                             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                                            / RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
CCLAUS        COMPUTE YMBPXF-PXFR-FEE-GST-AMT ROUNDED = 
CCLAUS            RSP06-PDI-PXFR-FEE-GST-AMT (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
CCLAUS                       / RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE   
           END-IF.  
CALFEE     IF RSP06-PXFR-TRUE-UP-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) = 1
CALFEE        MOVE 'Y'                   TO YMBPXF-TRUE-UP-IND  
CALFEE     ELSE 
CALFEE        MOVE 'N'                   TO YMBPXF-TRUE-UP-IND  
CALFEE     END-IF.  
CALFEE     IF RSP06-PXFR-GOVT-FEE-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) = 1   
CALFEE        MOVE 'Y'                   TO YMBPXF-GOVT-FEE-IND 
CALFEE     ELSE 
CALFEE        MOVE 'N'                   TO YMBPXF-GOVT-FEE-IND 
CALFEE     END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-SAIL-DATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) > 20111019 AND   
               RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) = 'F'
               IF  RSP06-PXFR-FEE-GROUP-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)
                   = 'T'
                   MOVE ZEROES           TO YMBPXF-TAX-FEE-AMT  
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
               WRITE-FX-REC 
CALFEE*        MOVE YMBPXF-REC           TO YMBPXFFX-REC
CALFEE         MOVE YMBPXF-REC (1:82)    TO YMBPXFFX-REC
               MOVE 'CCLPFF'             TO YMBPXFFX-RECORD-ID  
               MOVE '|'                  TO YMBPXFFX-FILLER-9   
                                            YMBPXFFX-FILLER-10  
CALFEE*                                     YMBPXFFX-FILLER-11  
CALFEE                                      YMBPXFFX-FILLER-12  
CALFEE                                      YMBPXFFX-FILLER-13  
               MOVE RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE  
                                         TO YMBPXFFX-FEE-XCHG-RATE  
CALFEE         IF RSP06-PXFR-TRUE-UP-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB) = 1
CALFEE            MOVE 'Y'               TO YMBPXFFX-TRUE-UP-IND
CALFEE         ELSE 
CALFEE            MOVE 'N'               TO YMBPXFFX-TRUE-UP-IND
CALFEE         END-IF   
CALFEE         IF RSP06-PXFR-GOVT-FEE-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PXFR-SUB)   
CALFEE                                                               = 1
CALFEE            MOVE 'Y'               TO YMBPXFFX-GOVT-FEE-IND   
CALFEE         ELSE 
CALFEE            MOVE 'N'               TO YMBPXFFX-GOVT-FEE-IND   
CALFEE         END-IF   
199974         PERFORM VARYING WS-CC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
199974                                UNTIL WS-CC-IDX > WS-YMBPXFFX-LEN 

199974           MOVE YMBPXFFX-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1)
199974                                     TO WS-ASCII  
199974           IF WS-BIN < 32 
199974              MOVE SPACES TO YMBPXFFX-REC(WS-CC-IDX:1)
199974           END-IF 
199974         END-PERFORM  
               WRITE YMBPXFFX-REC   
               ADD 1                     TO YMBPXFFX-CNT-REC-CNT
           ELSE 
199974         PERFORM VARYING WS-CC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
199974                                UNTIL WS-CC-IDX > WS-YMBPXF-LEN   
199974           MOVE YMBPXF-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1)  
199974                                     TO WS-ASCII  
199974           IF WS-BIN < 32 
199974              MOVE SPACES TO YMBPXF-REC(WS-CC-IDX:1)  
199974           END-IF 
199974         END-PERFORM  
               WRITE YMBPXF-REC 
               ADD 1                     TO YMBPXF-CNT-REC-CNT  
           END-IF.

AUSPOA     MOVE SPACES                   TO WS-STATE.   
AUSPOA  
AUSPOA     EXEC SQL 
AUSPOA          WHENEVER NOT FOUND

AUSPOA     END-EXEC.

AUSPOA           :RSP06-PIAR-STATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)

AUSPOA     END-EXEC.

AUSPOA 0900-NEXT-STEP.  
AUSPOA  
AUSPOA     EXEC SQL WHENEVER SQLERROR CONTINUE END-EXEC.
AUSPOA     EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.

AUSPOA     END-IF.

AUSPOA         MOVE 'US'                 TO WS-COUNTRY

           EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-STATUS (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
               WHEN 'D' 
                   MOVE 'DEPOSITED'      TO YMBPAX-STATUS-DESC  
               WHEN 'P' 
                   MOVE 'PAID IN FULL'   TO YMBPAX-STATUS-DESC  
               WHEN 'C' 
                   MOVE 'CANCELLED'      TO YMBPAX-STATUS-DESC  
               WHEN 'O' 
                   MOVE 'UNDER OPTION'   TO YMBPAX-STATUS-DESC  
               WHEN 'T' 
                   MOVE 'TICKETED'       TO YMBPAX-STATUS-DESC  
           END-EVALUATE.

           IF  RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  = 'I'   
               MOVE 'IR'                 TO   YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE

               EVALUATE GBR1-POLICY-TYPE (3:2)  
                   WHEN 'SA'  MOVE 'SA'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN 'IS'  MOVE 'IS'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN 'IN'  MOVE 'IN'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN 'LG'  MOVE 'LG'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN 'QD'  MOVE 'QD'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN 'AL'  MOVE 'AL'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
                   WHEN OTHER MOVE 'OP'  TO YMBPAX-BOOK-TYPE
               END-EVALUATE 
           END-IF.

191565*        COMPUTE YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT
191565*                = PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT
191565*                * RSP06-PAX-SINGLES-PCT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  
191565*    ELSE 
191565*        MOVE PMR1-OBC-AMOUNT      TO YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT   
191565*    END-IF.  

CCLAUS     MOVE ZEROES TO WS-OBC-AMT.   
161361     PERFORM VARYING WS-POBC-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
161361         UNTIL WS-POBC-SUB > RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT 
161361                                                   (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
161361         MOVE RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-POBC-SUB) 
161361                                   TO WS-OBC-CODE 
161361         PERFORM 3300-FETCH-OBC THRU  
161361                 3300-FETCH-OBC-EXIT  
161361         IF  OBC-CODE > SPACES
191565*            IF  OBC-PAYMENT-TYPE   = 'B'  OR 
191565*                OBC-INTERFACE-TYPE = 'P'  OR 
191565             IF  OBC-PAYMENT-TYPE NOT = 'A'  OR   
161361                 RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-POBC-SUB)  
161361                 = 'TRUP' 
161361                 CONTINUE 
161361             ELSE 
211671                 IF OBC-BV-ITEM-TYPE (4:2) = 30   
CCLAUS*                   COMPUTE YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT = YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT 
191565*                                       + RSP06-POBC-CHARGE-AMT   
CCLAUS                    COMPUTE WS-OBC-AMT = WS-OBC-AMT   
191565                                        + RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT  
161361                                       (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-POBC-SUB)
211671                 END-IF   
161361             END-IF   
161361         END-IF   
161361     END-PERFORM. 
CCLAUS     MOVE WS-OBC-AMT TO YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT.

PPC470*    IF SAIL1-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = 'USD'  
PPC470     IF TDP01-BASE-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = 'USD'  
CCLAUS        COMPUTE YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT ROUNDED =   
CCLAUS                YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT * RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE   
CCLAUS                / RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE  
PRCCMB        COMPUTE YMBPAX-FUN-PLAY-AMOUNT ROUNDED =  
PRCCMB                RSP06-PDI-FUN-PLAY-AMT (RSP06-PAX-SUB) *  
PRCCMB                RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE / 
PRCCMB                RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE
CCLAUS     END-IF.  
191565*    IF  RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE <= ZEROES  AND 
191565*        SSP13-O-NAV-BV-OBC-FLG = 0   
191565*         PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1 
191565*            UNTIL WS-SUB1 > BVP24-O-ENTRY-CNT
191565*           IF  BVP24-O-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-SUB1) 
191565*                              = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM(RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
191565*               ADD BVP24-O-PAX-OBC-AMT(WS-SUB1)  
191565*                                  TO YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT   
191565*               MOVE BVP24-O-ENTRY-CNT
191565*                                  TO WS-SUB1 
191565*           END-IF
191565*       END-PERFORM   
191565*    END-IF.  

           IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
               WRITE-FX-REC 
               COMPUTE YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT ROUNDED =  
CCLAUS*                YMBPAX-OBC-AMOUNT * RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE   
CCLAUS                 WS-OBC-AMT * RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE 
PRCCMB         COMPUTE YMBPAX-FUN-PLAY-AMOUNT ROUNDED = 
PRCCMB                 RSP06-PDI-FUN-PLAY-AMT (RSP06-PAX-SUB) * 
PRCCMB                 RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE  
           END-IF.  

PPC470*    IF  PMR1-OBC-CODE > SPACES   
PPC470*        MOVE PMR1-OBC-CODE        TO WS-OBC-CODE 
PPC470*        PERFORM 3300-FETCH-OBC THRU  
PPC470*                3300-FETCH-OBC-EXIT  
PPC470*        MOVE OBC-REFUND-IND       TO YMBPAX-OBC-REFUND-IND   
PPC470*        MOVE OBC-DEPT-CODE (1)    TO YMBPAX-OBC-DEPT-CODE
PPC470*    END-IF.  

156569*    MOVE PMR1-OBC-CODE            TO WS-OBC-CODE.
156569*    PERFORM 3300-FETCH-OBC THRU  
156569*            3300-FETCH-OBC-EXIT. 
156569* 
156569*    MOVE OBC-REFUND-IND           TO YMBPAX-OBC-REFUND-IND.  
156569*    MOVE OBC-DEPT-CODE (1)        TO YMBPAX-OBC-DEPT-CODE.   

161361*    MOVE PMR1-OBC-REFUND-IND      TO YMBPAX-OBC-REFUND-IND.  
161361*    MOVE PMR1-OBC-DEPT            TO YMBPAX-OBC-DEPT-CODE.   


           IF  RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  = 'I'   
               MOVE 'N'                  TO YMBPAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE
           ELSE 
               IF  RSP06-PAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  
                   > SPACES 
                   MOVE RSP06-PAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO YMBPAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 'N'              TO YMBPAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE
               END-IF   
               IF  RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  > ZERO
                   MOVE RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBPAX-SUGG-POINTS  
               ELSE 
                   MOVE 0                TO YMBPAX-SUGG-POINTS  
               END-IF   
               MOVE 0                    TO YMBPAX-REQ-SUGG-POINTS  
                                            WS-AMEN-RATE-ADJ-AMT
               IF  RSP06-PAX-INCL-AMENITY-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) NOT = 'N'
                   COMPUTE WS-AMEN-RATE-ADJ-AMT 
                           = GBR1-AMENITY-POINT-VALUE   
                           * GBR1-DURATION-DAYS 
                           * ( GBR1-OPTED-POINTS
                           -   RSP06-PAX-SUGG-POINTS (RSP06-PAX-SUB) )  
               END-IF   
               IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
                   WRITE-FX-REC 
                   COMPUTE YMBPAX-AMENITY-RATE-ADJ ROUNDED =
                           WS-AMEN-RATE-ADJ-AMT *   
                           RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE   
               ELSE 
                   MOVE WS-AMEN-RATE-ADJ-AMT
                                         TO YMBPAX-AMENITY-RATE-ADJ 
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP06-PAX-COP-INFO-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  > SPACE  
               MOVE RSP06-PAX-COP-INFO-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  
                                         TO YMBPAX-COP-INFO-IND 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-CONTRACT-IND        (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBPAX-CONTRACT-IND.
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-CONTRACT-ACCEPT-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBPAX-CONTRACT-ACCEPT-IND. 
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SAIL-SIGN-IND       (RSP06-PAX-SUB)

                                         TO YMBPAX-GUEST-INFO-IND.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-IMMIG-INFO-IND      (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBPAX-IMMIG-INFO-IND.  

           IF  RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND (RSP06-PAX-SUB) = 'Y'   
               IF  RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  > 0 
                   MOVE RSP06-PTFR-PRE-DECLINE-IND  (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO YMBPAX-PRE-TFR-DECLINE-IND  
                   MOVE RSP06-PTFR-POST-DECLINE-IND  (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
                                         TO YMBPAX-POST-TFR-DECLINE-IND 
                   MOVE 'N'              TO YMBPAX-PRE-SOT-TRANSFER 
                                            YMBPAX-POST-SOT-TRANSFER
                   PERFORM VARYING WS-PTFR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                       UNTIL WS-PTFR-SUB
                               > RSP06-PTFR-TRANSFER-CNT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)

                       IF  RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND  
                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB  WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'I' AND   
                           RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC  
                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB  WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'S'   
                           MOVE 'Y'      TO YMBPAX-PRE-SOT-TRANSFER 
                       END-IF   
                       IF  RSP06-PTFR-PRE-POST-IND  
                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB  WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'O' AND   
                           RSP06-PTFR-PURCHASE-SRC  
                           (RSP06-PAX-SUB  WS-PTFR-SUB) = 'S'   
                           MOVE 'Y'      TO  YMBPAX-POST-SOT-TRANSFER   
                       END-IF   
                   END-PERFORM  
               END-IF   
           END-IF.

PPC470*    EXEC SQL DECLARE TARGET_OFFER_TBL CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
PPC470*         SELECT OFFER_ID,
PPC470*                OFFER_NAME,  
PPC470*                EVENT_CODE,  
PPC470*                SEGMENT_NAME 
PPC470*           FROM TARGET_OFFER OFF 
PPC470*           JOIN TARGET_OFFER_MASTER MST  
PPC470*             ON OFF.OFFER_ID = MST.OFFER_ID  
PPC470*           LEFT JOIN TARGET_REDEMPTION RED   
PPC470*             ON (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = RED.IDENTIFIER_TYPE) AND  
PPC470*                (OFF.IDENTIFIER = RED.IDENTIFIER)           AND  
PPC470*                (OFF.OFFER_ID = RED.OFFER_ID)
PPC470*          WHERE (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = 'PG')                  AND
PPC470*                (OFF.IDENTIFIER =
PPC470*                   :RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB)) AND
PPC470*                (OFF.REDEMPTION_CNT > 0)                      AND
PPC470*                (RED.BOOK_NUM = :WS-PAX-BOOK-NUM)             AND
PPC470*                (RED.PAX_SEQ_NUM = :WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM)           AND
PPC470*                 (MST.RATE_CODE_1 =  
PPC470*                    :RSP06-PAX-RATE-CODE (RSP06-PAX-SUB))
PPC470*    END-EXEC.
PPC470*

PPC470*         OPEN TARGET_OFFER_TBL   
PPC470*    END-EXEC.

PPC470*         GO TO 1620-CLOSE-CURSOR 
PPC470*    END-EXEC.

PPC470*             :WSTOMR-OFFER-NAME, 
PPC470*             :WSTOMR-EVENT-CODE, 
PPC470*             :WSTOMR-SEGMENT-NAME
PPC470*    END-EXEC.

PPC470                 EVENT_CODE,  
PPC470                 SEGMENT_NAME 
PPC470            INTO :WSTOMR-OFFER-ID,
PPC470                 :WSTOMR-OFFER-NAME,

PPC470                 (OFF.IDENTIFIER =
PPC470                    :RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB)) AND

PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC.

       1700-PROCESS-INSURANCE.

           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE      TO YMBPAX-INS-REQUEST-DATE.

           IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
               WRITE-FX-REC 
               COMPUTE YMBPAX-INS-PREMIUM-AMT = RSP06-TIR-PREMIUM-AMT   
                                                (RSP06-PAX-SUB) *   
                                                RSP06-IBR-PRC-XCHG-RATE 
           ELSE 
               MOVE RSP06-TIR-PREMIUM-AMT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBPAX-INS-PREMIUM-AMT  
           END-IF.  

           MULTIPLY YMBPAX-INS-PREMIUM-AMT  
                 BY RSP06-PAX-INSURANCE-COMM-PCT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
             GIVING YMBPAX-INS-COMM-AMT.

       1700-PROCESS-INSURANCE-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       1800-PROCESS-CPA.

       1900-PROCESS-CABIN.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-CABIN-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO WS-PAX-CABIN-NUM.   

           IF  WS-PAX-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'W' OR 'Q' OR 'C'
               GO TO 1910-PROCESS-TBA   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-PAX-CABIN-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO DPP34-CABIN-NUM.
           MOVE SPACES                   TO DPP34-EDIT-TYPE.

           PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-CABIN THRU   
                   DPP33-EXIT.  

           IF  DPP34-ERROR-SUB > 0  
               GO TO 1930-CABIN-DESC-ERROR  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY  TO WS-NORMAL-CAPACITY. 
           MOVE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY     TO WS-MAXIMUM-CAPACITY.

           GO TO 1900-PROCESS-CABIN-EXIT.   

       1910-PROCESS-TBA.

           MOVE YMBPAX-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE TO DPP34-SELECT-CATEGORY-CODE. 
           MOVE SPACES                   TO DPP34-EDIT-TYPE.

           PERFORM DPP33-FETCH-AVAIL-RECORD THRU
                   DPP33-EXIT.  

           IF  DPP34-ERROR-SUB > 0  
               GO TO 1920-AVAIL-CABIN-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE DPP34-NORMAL-CAPACITY    TO WS-NORMAL-CAPACITY. 
           MOVE DPP34-MAX-CAPACITY       TO WS-MAXIMUM-CAPACITY.

           GO TO 1900-PROCESS-CABIN-EXIT.   

       1920-AVAIL-CABIN-ERROR.  

           DISPLAY ' ERR NUM  = '   ERROR-NUM   
                   ' ERR STAT = '   ERROR-STATUS
                   ' ERR AREA = '   ERROR-AREA        UPON PRINTER. 

           DISPLAY 'ERROR ON AVAIL CAB REC FETCH'     UPON PRINTER. 

           STRING 'ERROR ON FETCH OF AVAIL CABIN REC FOR '  
                   RSP06-PAX-CABIN-NUM  (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
               ' ' RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM   (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
               ' ' RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE  (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
               DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO YMBERR-LINE.  

           WRITE YMBERR-REC.

           DISPLAY 'PARAM = ' PARAM-BOOK-NUM          UPON PRINTER. 

           MOVE 'Y'                      TO WS-ERROR-SW.

           GO TO 1900-PROCESS-CABIN-EXIT.   

       1930-CABIN-DESC-ERROR.   

           DISPLAY ' ERR NUM  = '   ERROR-NUM   
                   ' ERR STAT = '   ERROR-STATUS
                   ' ERR AREA = '   ERROR-AREA        UPON PRINTER. 

           DISPLAY 'ERROR ON CABIN DESC REC FETCH'    UPON PRINTER. 

           STRING 'ERROR ON FETCH OF CABIN DESC REC FOR '

           WRITE YMBERR-REC.

           MOVE 'Y'                      TO WS-ERROR-SW.

       1900-PROCESS-CABIN-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

188273*** UNCOMMENTING THE FETCH BELOW.  STAYING ON WRONG SAILING   
188273** AND FAILING IN DPP80 IF AN OLD PAX WAS CANCELLED ON DIFFERENT SAIL 
188273** USING A CAT CODE THAT DOES NOT EXIST ON CURRENT SHIP!  THIS
188273* STEP MIGHT ONLY BE NECESSARY FOR A SHIP CHANGE SINCE PRIMARILY  
188273* TRYING TO GET A CATEGORY MATCH IN DPP80.

199974                    END-IF

                       WRITE YMBPPK-REC

                   END-IF   
               END-IF

PPC470 3500-PROCESS-GUEST-OFFER.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF ((RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM (WS-TSUB) = 
PPC470          RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) OR 0) AND 
PPC470         (RSP06-OFFER-ID (WS-TSUB) =  
PPC470          RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (RSP06-PAX-SUB)))
PPC470         CONTINUE 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         GO TO 3500-PROCESS-GUEST-OFFER-EXIT  
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     IF  RSP06-COUPON-CODE (WS-TSUB) > SPACES AND 
PPC470         RSP06-COUPON-CODE (WS-TSUB) NOT = '~'
PPC470         MOVE 'Y'        TO WS-COUPON-EXIST   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-T2SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
PPC470         UNTIL WS-T2SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT(WS-TSUB)   
PPC470         IF (WS-COUPON-EXIST = 'Y')                        AND
PPC470            (RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) = 'ADB') AND
PPC470            (RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) (1:1) = 
PPC470                 'Y' OR 'Z')  
PPC470             MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)
PPC470                                      TO YMBPAX-COUPON-ENTITY-CODE
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470  
PPC470         IF  RSP06-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) > 2  
PPC470             PERFORM 3600-FIND-XTA-PRICING THRU   
PPC470                     3600-FIND-XTA-PRICING-EXIT   
PPC470         END-IF   

PPC470         IF RSP06-OBC-ITEM-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) > SPACES   
PPC470             MOVE RSP06-OBC-ITEM-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)  
PPC470                                      TO WS-OBC-ITEM-CODE 
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470  
PPC470         IF  RSP06-TARGETED-IND (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) = 'Y'  
PPC470             MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)
PPC470                                      TO WS-TOMR-OFFER-ENTITY-CODE
PPC470         END-IF   

PPC470         IF (RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM(WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) > '000000')   
PPC470             PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
PPC470                 UNTIL WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-NUM (WS-SUB) =
PPC470                    RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) OR
PPC470                    WS-SUB > WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-CNT 
PPC470                 CONTINUE 
PPC470             END-PERFORM  
PPC470  
PPC470             IF  WS-SUB > WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-CNT
PPC470                 ADD 1                TO WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-CNT 
PPC470                 MOVE WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-CNT
PPC470                                      TO WS-SUB   
PPC470                 MOVE RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM      (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)
PPC470                                      TO WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-NUM 
PPC470                                                          (WS-SUB)
PPC470                 MOVE ZEROES          TO WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT(WS-SUB)
PPC470             END-IF   
PPC470         END-IF   

PPC470         EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470           WHEN 1 
PPC470              IF  RSP06-POS-1-ADJUSTMENT-AMT(WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) > 0
PPC470                  ADD RSP06-POS-1-ADJUSTMENT-AMT(WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)
PPC470                                      TO WS-SUPP-AMT-RPT  
PPC470              ELSE
PPC470                  IF  RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) >   
PPC470                                                          '000000'
PPC470                      ADD RSP06-POS-1-ADJUSTMENT-AMT  
PPC470                                          (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)  
PPC470                                      TO WS-DISC-AMT-RPT  
PPC470                      MULTIPLY WS-DISC-AMT-RPT BY -1  
PPC470                          GIVING WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUB)   
PPC470                  END-IF  
PPC470              END-IF  
PPC470           WHEN 2 
PPC470              IF  RSP06-POS-2-ADJUSTMENT-AMT(WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) > 0
PPC470                  ADD RSP06-POS-2-ADJUSTMENT-AMT(WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)
PPC470                                      TO WS-SUPP-AMT-RPT  
PPC470              ELSE
PPC470                  IF  RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) >   
PPC470                                                          '000000'
PPC470                      ADD RSP06-POS-2-ADJUSTMENT-AMT  
PPC470                                          (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)  
PPC470                                      TO WS-DISC-AMT-RPT  
PPC470                      MULTIPLY WS-DISC-AMT-RPT BY -1  
PPC470                          GIVING WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUB)   
PPC470                  END-IF  
PPC470              END-IF

PPC470 3600-FIND-XTA-PRICING.   

PPC470     IF  RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)       = 'ADB' AND   
PPC470         RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB) (1:2) = 'EG'
PPC470         MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)  
PPC470                                      TO WS-ADB-OWN-ID
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF  RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)       = 'RTE'   
PPC470         MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (WS-TSUB WS-T2SUB)  
PPC470                                      TO WS-RTE-OWN-ID
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470 3600-FIND-XTA-PRICING-EXIT.  
PPC470     EXIT.

PPC470 3700-BUILD-PRC-FRM-RTE.  

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          SELECT  PHASE_NUM,  
PPC470                  RATE_CODE   
PPC470             INTO :WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM,
PPC470                  :WS-RATE-CODE   
PPC470            FROM  RATE_MASTER 
PPC470           WHERE  PRP_OWNER_ID   = :WS-RTE-OWN-ID AND 
PPC470                  PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'RTE'  
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470         GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470     MOVE WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM.   
PPC470     MOVE WS-RATE-CODE (2:2)         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-CODE.
PPC470     MOVE WS-RATE-CODE (1:1)         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-TYPE.

PPC470 3700-BUILD-PRC-FRM-RTE-EXIT. 
PPC470     EXIT.

PPC470 3800-BUILD-PRC-FRM-ADB.  

PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470          SELECT PHASE_NUM,   
PPC470                 ADD_BENEFIT_CODE 
PPC470            INTO :WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM, 
PPC470                 :WS-OWNER-CODE   
PPC470            FROM  ADD_BENEFIT_MASTER  
PPC470            WHERE PRP_OWNER_ID   = :WS-ADB-OWN-ID 
PPC470            AND   PRP_OWNER_CODE = 'ADB'  
PPC470            AND   ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE = 'E'  
PPC470           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
PPC470     END-EXEC 
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470         GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470     MOVE WS-PRICE-PHASE-NUM         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-PHASE-NUM.   
PPC470     MOVE WS-OWNER-CODE(3:1)         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-TYPE.
PPC470     MOVE WS-OWNER-CODE(4:2)         TO YMBPAX-PRICE-CODE.

PPC470 3800-BUILD-PRC-FRM-ADB-EXIT. 
PPC470     EXIT.

195636 4000-FETCH-FIRST-PAX-CXF.
195636     FIND CURRENT INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD.

195636     FETCH FIRST PAX-CXF-REC WITHIN   
195636         INDV-BOOKING-PAX-SET SET.

195636     ADD 1                         TO WS-PAX-CXF-CNT. 

195636 4100-FETCH-NEXT-PAX-CXF. 

195636     FETCH NEXT PAX-CXF-REC WITHIN INDV-BOOKING-PAX-SET SET   
195636           AT END GO TO 4999-EXIT.

195636     ADD 1                         TO WS-PAX-CXF-CNT. 

195636     IF WS-PAX-CXF-CNT < 11   
195636        GO TO 4100-FETCH-NEXT-PAX-CXF 
195636     END-IF.  

195636     IF  RSP06-IBR-CURRENCY-CODE NOT = SSP05-O-CURRENCY-CODE  
195636         MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-MULTI-CURR-BOOK  
195636                                      WS-WRITE-FX-REC 
195636     END-IF.  

195636     MOVE 1                        TO WS-CXP-SUB. 
195636     MOVE PAX-REC                  TO RSP06-CXP-PAX-REC   
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE ZERO                     TO RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636                                      RSP06-CPPR-CNT (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636     MOVE 'N'                      TO RSP06-CTIR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636                                      RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND   
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636                                      RSP06-PXFR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636                                      RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE LOW-VALUES               TO RSP06-TPR-REC  (WS-CXP-SUB) 
105636                                      RSP06-CPPR-INFO(WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4200-BUILD-RSP06-CXP-VIA-PCX.
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-ADDON-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =   
195636             RSP06-PCX-ADDON-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *   
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-CRUISE-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =  
195636             RSP06-PCX-CRUISE-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *  
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-DISCOUNT-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =
195636             RSP06-PCX-DISCOUNT-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-PACKAGE-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED = 
195636             RSP06-PCX-PACKAGE-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) * 
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-PTCHG-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =   
195636             RSP06-PCX-PTCHG-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *   
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-TRANSFER-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =
195636             RSP06-PCX-TRANSFER-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-TRNS-GST-PCT.  
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-ADDON-COMM-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =  
195636             RSP06-PCX-ADDON-COMM-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *  
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-PACKAGE-COMM-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =
195636             RSP06-PCX-PACKAGE-COMM-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-XFR-COMM-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =
195636             RSP06-PCX-TRANSFER-COMM-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *   
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT.   
195636     COMPUTE RSP06-CXP-INS-COMM-GST-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) ROUNDED =
195636             RSP06-PCX-INSURANCE-COMM-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) *  
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT .  

195636     MOVE ZEROES                   TO RSP06-CXP-TOTAL-INS-GST-AMT 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636                                    RSP06-CXP-CRUISE-SUPP-GST-AMT 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4300-BUILD-RSP06-CXP-VIA-WS. 
195636     MOVE RSP06-PCX-SEQ-NUM (WS-CXP-SUB)  
195636                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM. 

195636     PERFORM 6000-PROCESS-PENALTY-DATA THRU 6099-EXIT.

195636     MOVE WS-PENALTY-GST-AMT       TO RSP06-CXP-PENALTY-GST-AMT   
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE WS-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT      TO RSP06-CXP-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT  
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE WS-PAX-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT TO RSP06-CXP-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE WS-PAX-CREDIT-GST-AMT    TO RSP06-CXP-CREDIT-GST-AMT
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE WS-PAX-MISC-GST-AMT      TO RSP06-CXP-MISC-GST-AMT  
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4400-FETCH-TRAVEL-INS-REC.   
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-BOOK-NUM         TO TIR-BOOK-NUM.   
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-SEQ-NUM          TO TIR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.

195636     FETCH TRAVEL-INS-REC RECORD  
195636          ON ERROR GO TO 4500-FETCH-PAX-PHONE-REC.

195636     MOVE 'Y'                      TO RSP06-CTIR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE TRAVEL-INS-REC           TO 
195636                                  RSP06-CXP-TIR-INFO (WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4500-FETCH-PAX-PHONE-REC.
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-BOOK-NUM         TO PPHOR-BOOK-NUM. 
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-SEQ-NUM          TO PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.  

195636     MOVE 'PAXPHONE '              TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA.  

195636     EVALUATE PPHOR-BOOK-NUM (6:1)
195636        WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN '0'
195636              MOVE '0'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '1'
195636              MOVE '1'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN '2'
195636              MOVE '2'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '3'
195636              MOVE '3'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4'
195636              MOVE '4'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5'
195636              MOVE '5'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6'
195636              MOVE '6'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'
195636              MOVE '7'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'
195636              MOVE '8'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636        WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9'
195636              MOVE '9'             TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1) 
195636     END-EVALUATE.

195636     FETCH PAX-PHONE-REC RECORD   
195636          ON ERROR GO TO 4700-FETCH-PAX-FEE-REC.  

195636     MOVE 'Y'                      TO RSP06-PPHOR-EXIST-IND   
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE PAX-PHONE-REC            TO RSP06-PAX-PHONE-REC 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4700-FETCH-PAX-FEE-REC.  
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-BOOK-NUM         TO PXFR-BOOK-NUM.  
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-SEQ-NUM          TO PXFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.   

195636     FETCH PAX-FEE-REC RECORD 
195636          ON ERROR GO TO 4800-FETCH-PAX-TRANSFER-REC. 

195636     MOVE 'Y'                      TO RSP06-PXFR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE PAX-FEE-REC              TO RSP06-PXFR-INFO 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
195636               UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP06-PXFR-CNT  (WS-CXP-SUB)
195636       IF RSP06-PXFR-GOVT-FEE-IND (WS-CXP-SUB WS-SUB) = 1 
195636         ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB WS-SUB) TO
195636             RSP06-PDI-TOTAL-FEE-GOVT-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB)
195636       ELSE   
195636         ADD RSP06-PXFR-FEE-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB WS-SUB) TO
195636             RSP06-PDI-TOTAL-FEE-OTH-AMT (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636       END-IF 
195636     END-PERFORM. 

195636 4800-FETCH-PAX-TRANSFER-REC. 
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-BOOK-NUM         TO PTFR-BOOK-NUM.  
195636     MOVE PAX-CXF-SEQ-NUM          TO PTFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.   

195636     FETCH PAX-TRANSFER-REC RECORD
195636         ON ERROR GO TO 4900-WRITE-CXP-PAX.   

195636     MOVE 'Y'                      TO RSP06-PTFR-EXIST-IND
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).
195636     MOVE PAX-TRANSFER-REC         TO RSP06-PTFR-INFO 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB).

195636 4900-WRITE-CXP-PAX.  
195636     PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-CXP-PAX THRU
195636             2600-PROCESS-CXP-PAX-EXIT.   
195636     IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK  
195636         MOVE SPACE                TO WS-WRITE-FX-REC 
195636                                      WS-ERROR-SW 
195636         MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-PROCESS-BLENDED  
195636  
195636         MOVE TRAVEL-INS-REC       TO RSP06-CXP-TIR-INFO  
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636         MOVE PAX-PHONE-REC        TO RSP06-PAX-PHONE-REC 
195636                                                     (WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636         MOVE PAX-FEE-REC          TO RSP06-PXFR-INFO(WS-CXP-SUB) 
195636         MOVE PAX-TRANSFER-REC     TO RSP06-PTFR-INFO(WS-CXP-SUB) 

195636         PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-CXP-PAX THRU
195636                 2600-PROCESS-CXP-PAX-EXIT
195636     END-IF.  

195636     GO TO 4100-FETCH-NEXT-PAX-CXF.   

195636 4999-EXIT.   
195636     EXIT.

195636 6000-PROCESS-PENALTY-DATA.   
195636     COPY IRP64INT.   
195636     MOVE ZEROES                   TO WS-PAX-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT
195636                                      WS-PAX-CREDIT-GST-AMT   
195636                                      WS-PAX-MISC-GST-AMT 
195636                                      WS-PAX-CPN-GST-AMT  
195636                                      WS-PENALTY-GST-AMT  
195636                                      WS-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT 
195636                                      WS-PEN-GST-AMT  
195636                                      WS-MAR-AMT. 

195636     MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM             TO IRP64-I-BOOK-NUM.   

195636     PERFORM IRP64-RETURN-MAR THRU
195636             IRP64-RETURN-MAR-EXIT.   

195636     IF IRP64-END-OF-MAR OR IRP64-SQL-ERROR   
195636        GO TO 6099-EXIT   
195636     END-IF.  

195636     GO TO 6050-PROCESS-MAR.  

195636 6025-PROCESS-NEXT-MAR.   
195636     MOVE IBR-BOOK-NUM             TO IRP64-I-BOOK-NUM

195636     PERFORM IRP64-RETURN-MAR THRU
195636             IRP64-RETURN-MAR-EXIT.   

195636     IF IRP64-END-OF-MAR OR IRP64-SQL-ERROR   
195636        GO TO 6099-EXIT   
195636     END-IF.  

195636 6050-PROCESS-MAR.
195636     IF IRP64-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'A' OR
195636        (WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM NOT = IRP64-PAX-SEQ-NUM)  
195636        GO TO 6025-PROCESS-NEXT-MAR   
195636     END-IF.  

195636     MULTIPLY IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY BY IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT 
195636              GIVING WS-MAR-AMT.  

195636     IF RSP23-I-CONV-RATE-BASE = 'C'  
195636        MOVE 'Y'                   TO WS-APPLY-MAR-XCHG-IND   
195636     ELSE 
195636        MOVE 'N'                   TO WS-APPLY-MAR-XCHG-IND   
195636     END-IF.  

195636     EVALUATE IRP64-TYPE  
195636       WHEN 'P'   
195636         IF (IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT > ZEROES) AND  
195636            (IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT < (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY *  
195636                               IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT))  
195636            COMPUTE WS-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
195636            (IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT * RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-TRNS-GST-PCT) +
195636            ((IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT) -   
195636              IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT) * RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT  

195636            COMPUTE WS-PCOMM-GST-AMT ROUNDED =
195636                    IRP64-COMM-AMT * RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT
195636         ELSE 
195636            COMPUTE WS-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
195636            (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT * 
195636             RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT )  
195636            COMPUTE WS-PCOMM-GST-AMT ROUNDED  =   
195636                    IRP64-COMM-AMT * RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT
195636         END-IF   

195636         IF WS-APPLY-MAR-XCHG-IND = 'Y'   
195636            COMPUTE WS-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED  = WS-PEN-GST-AMT /
195636                                              IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE 
195636            COMPUTE WS-PCOMM-GST-AMT ROUNDED =
195636                    WS-PCOMM-GST-AMT / IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE
195636         END-IF   

195636         ADD WS-PEN-GST-AMT TO WS-PENALTY-GST-AMT 
195636         ADD WS-PCOMM-GST-AMT TO WS-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT  

195636       WHEN 'A'   
195636             COMPUTE WS-PAX-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT ROUNDED =   
195636                     WS-PAX-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT + (WS-MAR-AMT * 
195636                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT )  
195636       WHEN 'C'   
195636             COMPUTE WS-PAX-CREDIT-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
195636                     WS-PAX-CREDIT-GST-AMT + (WS-MAR-AMT *
195636                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT )  
195636       WHEN 'D'   
195636             COMPUTE WS-PAX-MISC-GST-AMT ROUNDED =
195636                     WS-PAX-MISC-GST-AMT  +  (WS-MAR-AMT *
195636                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT )  
195636       WHEN 'S'   
195636             COMPUTE WS-PAX-CPN-GST-AMT ROUNDED = 
195636                     WS-PAX-CPN-GST-AMT  +  (WS-MAR-AMT * 
195636                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT )  
195636     END-EVALUATE.

195636     GO TO 6025-PROCESS-NEXT-MAR. 

195636 6099-EXIT.   
195636     EXIT.

105206 D100-PAX-FCC-MAMR.   
105206      EXEC SQL DECLARE MISC_AMOUNT_CURSOR 
105206               CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
105206           SELECT MA.CODE,
105206                  MA.ACTUAL_AMOUNT
105206             FROM FCC_REDEMPTION_TBL FR   
105206             JOIN MISC_AMOUNT MA  
105206               ON MA.MAR_ID = FR.MAR_ID   
105206            WHERE FR.BOOK_NUM =   
105206                     :RSP06-PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM(RSP06-PAX-SUB) AND 
105206                  FR.PAX_SEQ_NUM =
105206                     :RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM(RSP06-PAX-SUB)
105206      END-EXEC.   
105206  
105206      EXEC SQL
105206          OPEN MISC_AMOUNT_CURSOR 
105206      END-EXEC.   
105206  
105206      EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND 
105206          GO TO D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT-EXIT 
105206      END-EXEC.   

105206      PERFORM D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT THRU   
105206              D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT-EXIT.  
105206  
105206      EXEC SQL
105206          CLOSE MISC_AMOUNT_CURSOR
105206      END-EXEC.   
105206  
105206 D100-PAX-FCC-MAMR-EXIT.  
105206      EXIT.   
105206  
105206  
105206 D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT.
105206        EXEC SQL  
105206             FETCH NEXT FROM MISC_AMOUNT_CURSOR   
105206             INTO :WS-MAMR-CODE,  
105206                  :WS-ACTUAL-AMT  
105206        END-EXEC. 

105206        IF SQLCODE < 0
105206            GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
105206        END-IF.   

105206        IF SQLCODE = 100  
105206            GO TO D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT-EXIT   
105206        END-IF.   
105206  
105206        MOVE WS-MAMR-CODE TO MAMR-MISC-CHARGE-CODE
105206        FETCH MISC-AMOUNT-MASTER-REC RECORD.  
105206  
105206        IF MAMR-FCC-IND = 0   
105206           COMPUTE WS-PAX-NON-ACCR-RDM-AMT =  
105206                   WS-PAX-NON-ACCR-RDM-AMT + WS-ACTUAL-AMT
105206        ELSE  
105206           COMPUTE WS-PAX-ACCR-RDM-AMT     =  
105206                   WS-PAX-ACCR-RDM-AMT     + WS-ACTUAL-AMT
105206        END-IF.   
105206  
105206        GO TO D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT.   
105206  
105206 D150-CAL-PAX-FCC-AMT-EXIT.   
105206      EXIT.   
105206  
105206 D200-FIND-REDEEMED-FCC.  
105206     MOVE ZEROS TO WS-FCC-REDEEMED-CNT
105206                   WS-PAX-NON-ACCR-RDM-AMT
105206                   WS-PAX-ACCR-RDM-AMT.   
105206  
105206  
105206     EXEC SQL SELECT COUNT(*) 
105206                INTO :WS-FCC-REDEEMED-CNT 
105206              FROM FCC_REDEMPTION_TBL 
105206             WHERE BOOK_NUM = 
105206                 :RSP06-PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) AND
105206                  PAX_SEQ_NUM =   
105206                 :RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) AND   
105206                  FCC_AMT IS NOT NULL 
105206     END-EXEC.
105206  
105206     EXEC SQL WHENEVER SQLERROR   
105206          GO TO D200-REDEEMED-FCC-ERROR   
105206     END-EXEC.
105206  
105206     EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND  
105206          GO TO D200-FIND-REDEEMED-FCC-EXIT   
105206     END-EXEC.
105206  
105206     GO TO D200-FIND-REDEEMED-FCC-EXIT.   
105206  
105206 D200-REDEEMED-FCC-ERROR. 
105206     MOVE 35 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX. 
105206     GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.   
105206  
105206 D200-FIND-REDEEMED-FCC-EXIT. 
105206     EXIT.

148695 8900-PROCESS-PPHOR.

148695                                        WS-PPHOR-MOB-SWITCH

148695     MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-BOOK-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
148695                                     TO  YMBPICR-PIAR-BOOK-NUM.   
148695     MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PAX-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
148695                                     TO  YMBPICR-PIAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.
148695     MOVE RSP06-PAX-BOOK-TYPE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
148695                                     TO  YMBPICR-PIAR-BOOK-TYPE.  
148695     MOVE RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-FIRST-NAME.  
148695     MOVE RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-LAST-NAME.   
148695     MOVE 'SELF'                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-RELATIONSHIP.
148695     MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-PHONE-CNT.   

148695     PERFORM VARYING WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL 
148695       WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB > RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-CNT (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
148695         EVALUATE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE  
148695           (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB) 
148695           WHEN 'H'   
148695             MOVE WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB TO WS-PPHOR-HOME-SWITCH  
148695           WHEN 'C'   
148695             MOVE WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB TO WS-PPHOR-MOB-SWITCH   
148695         END-EVALUATE 
148695         MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-TYPE  
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB)
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-PHONE-TYPE   
148695         MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM   
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB)
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-PICR-PHONE-NUM
148695         MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB)
148695                                     TO   
148695                                 YMBPICR-PICR-SEND-TXT-MSG-IND
148695         MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-SEND-TXT-PREF-IND   
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB)
148695                                     TO YMBPICR-SEND-TEXT-PREF-IND
148695         IF RSP06-PPHOR-SAIL-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB) = 1
148695            MOVE 'Y'                 TO YMBPICR-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG
148695         ELSE 
148695            MOVE 'N'                 TO YMBPICR-SAIL-TEXT-OPT-FLAG
148695         END-IF   
148695         IF RSP06-PPHOR-PAYM-TXT-OPT-FLG  
148695                        (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-PHONE-SUB) = 1
148695            MOVE 'Y'                 TO YMBPICR-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG
148695         ELSE 
148695            MOVE 'N'                 TO YMBPICR-PAYM-TEXT-OPT-FLAG
148695         END-IF   
199974         PERFORM VARYING WS-CC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
199974                        UNTIL WS-CC-IDX > WS-YMBPICR-LEN  
199974            MOVE YMBPICR-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1)
199974                                      TO WS-ASCII 
199974            IF WS-BIN < 32
199974               MOVE SPACES TO YMBPICR-REC(WS-CC-IDX:1)
199974            END-IF
199974         END-PERFORM  
148695         WRITE YMBPICR-REC
148695         ADD 1                       TO YMBPICR-CNT-REC-CNT   
148695     END-PERFORM. 
148695     IF  WS-PPHOR-MOB-SWITCH > ZEROES 
148695         MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM   
148695             (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-MOB-SWITCH)  
148695                                     TO WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE   
148695         MOVE 'MO'                   TO YMBPAX-PHONE-NUM-TYPE 
148695         ELSE 
148695           IF  WS-PPHOR-HOME-SWITCH > ZEROES  
148695               MOVE RSP06-PPHOR-PHONE-NUM 
148695                   (RSP06-PAX-SUB WS-PPHOR-HOME-SWITCH)   
148695                                     TO WS-HOLD-PPHOR-PHONE   
148695               MOVE 'HM'             TO YMBPAX-PHONE-NUM-TYPE 
148695           END-IF 
148695     END-IF.  
148695     MOVE WS-PPHOR-PHONE             TO YMBPAX-PHONE-NUM. 

148695 8900-EXIT.   
148695     EXIT.


       9000-END-OF-JOB.

               MOVE ERROR-NO-ERRORS-LINE TO ERROR-D-LINE
               PERFORM 12000-ERROR-LINE THRU
                       12000-ERROR-LINE-EXIT
           END-IF.

               MOVE 5                    TO ERROR-PRINT-LINE-COUNTER

                                         TO ERROR-HDG1-PAGE-OUT 
               WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG1 
               WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG2 
               WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG3 
               WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-UNDER-LINE   
               MOVE  SPACES              TO YMBERR-REC  
               WRITE YMBERR-REC 
           END-IF.

PPC470 9988-BUILD-PAXGLA-FILE.  
PPC470     MOVE 'CCLGLA'                 TO YMBGLA-RECORD-ID.   
PPC470     MOVE WS-DATESTAMP             TO YMBGLA-DATESTAMP.   
PPC470     MOVE '|'                      TO 
PPC470     YMBGLA-FILLER-1    YMBGLA-FILLER-2    YMBGLA-FILLER-3
PPC470     YMBGLA-FILLER-4    YMBGLA-FILLER-5    YMBGLA-FILLER-6
PPC470     YMBGLA-FILLER-7    YMBGLA-FILLER-8    YMBGLA-FILLER-9.   

PPC470     MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-OFFER-ID.
PPC470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM       TO YMBGLA-BOOK-NUM.
PPC470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-GROUP-BOOK-NUM TO YMBGLA-GRP-BOOK-NUM.
PPC470     MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB)   
                                         TO YMBGLA-PAX-SEQ-NUM. 
PPC470     MOVE RSP06-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
                                         TO YMBGLA-SEQ-IN-CABIN.

PPC470     IF  RSP06-PAX-BOOK-DATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  > 1000000   
PPC470         MOVE RSP06-PAX-BOOK-DATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-BOOK-DATE
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         IF  RSP06-PAX-BOOK-DATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB)  > 0 
PPC470             MOVE RSP06-PAX-BOOK-DATE (RSP06-PAX-SUB) 
PPC470                                   TO 
PPC470                                   WS-HOLD-YYYYMMDD-DATE  
PPC470             MOVE WS-HOLD-YYMMDD-DATE 
PPC470                                   TO DPP02-YYMMDD-DATE   
PPC470             PERFORM DPP10-YYMMDD-TO-YYYYMMDD 
PPC470             MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-BOOK-DATE
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     MOVE RSP06-PAX-CANCEL-DATE    (RSP06-PAX-SUB)
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-CANCEL-DATE. 
PPC470     MOVE WS-OFFER-DISC-GL-ACC-NUM(WS-SUBG)   
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-GL-ACCT-NUM. 
PPC470     MOVE WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUBG) 
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLA-GL-DISC-AMT. 

PPC470     IF  ( MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND
PPC470          WRITE-FX-REC )  OR  
PPC470          ( NOT MULTI-CURR-BOOK ) 
PPC470          CONTINUE
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470           COMPUTE WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUBG) =  
PPC470                   WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUBG) /  
PPC470                   RSP06-IB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE 
PPC470     END-IF.  


PPC470     IF  MULTI-CURR-BOOK AND  
               WRITE-FX-REC 
               MOVE YMBGLA-REC           TO YMBGLAFX-REC
PPC470         MOVE 'CCLGLF'             TO YMBGLAFX-RECORD-ID  
PPC470         MOVE WS-OFFER-DISC-AMT (WS-SUBG) 
PPC470                                   TO YMBGLAFX-GL-DISC-AMT
199974         PERFORM VARYING WS-CC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
199974                            UNTIL WS-CC-IDX > WS-YMBGLAFX-LEN 
199974            MOVE YMBGLAFX-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1)   
199974                                   TO WS-ASCII
199974            IF WS-BIN < 32
199974               MOVE SPACES         TO YMBGLAFX-REC(WS-CC-IDX:1)   
199974            END-IF
199974         END-PERFORM  
PPC470         WRITE YMBGLAFX-REC   
PPC470         ADD 1                     TO YMBGLAFX-CNT-REC-CNT
PPC470     ELSE 
199974         PERFORM VARYING WS-CC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
199974                              UNTIL WS-CC-IDX > WS-YMBGLA-LEN 
199974            MOVE YMBGLA-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1) 
199974                                   TO WS-ASCII
199974            IF WS-BIN < 32
199974               MOVE SPACES         TO YMBGLA-REC(WS-CC-IDX:1) 
199974            END-IF
199974         END-PERFORM  
PPC470         WRITE YMBGLA-REC 
PPC470         ADD 1                     TO YMBGLA-CNT-REC-CNT  
PPC470     END-IF.
